01. Attribute and Skill System

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 2,5,6,8,11,14,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Attribute meaning and independent development

The latest t99 working direction uses Strength, Dexterity, Constitution, Intelligence, Wisdom, Charisma and Attunement. Baseline10, approximately20 for an average Tier9 and potentially30 for an exceptional Tier9 are creator working directions, not a fixed distributable point pool. The older six Force/Precision/Endurance/Cunning/Presence/Insight are retained in complete cb94/738f records, not silently converted. Definitions below and every new coefficient are PROPOSALS.

Strength measures force and load bearing, not proficiency. Dexterity measures coordination and control, not knowledge. Constitution supports sustained exertion and resilience, not immunity. Intelligence concerns reasoning and structured learning, not access to truth. Wisdom concerns attention and judgment under incomplete evidence, not omniscience. Charisma concerns expression and interpersonal influence, not compulsory obedience. Attunement concerns affinity access/control capacity, not ownership of concepts or permission to use every technique. Current effective scores may change with injury or equipment; permanent growth and advancement eligibility do not inherit temporary bonuses.

Learning, suppression and meaningful practice

A learned skill retains its earned XP when its associated core is suppressed. Effective use is capped by the current core through F04. F02 gives cumulative XP; F03 awards qualifying practice with challenge and feedback, including reduced qualifying-failure credit. Repeating an event ID awards nothing. Novelty, recovery and time-window checks precede credit, so hitting an inert target forever is not a growth engine. A temporary score increase may improve current execution but never manufactures historical XP or mastery evidence.

F29 selects an eligible training opportunity, not an immediate attribute increase. Archetype weights influence available opportunities rather than assigning a fixed sum of scores. F30 requires recovery from strain. F31 spends an external XP bank sequentially and recomputes its cost after each point, respecting a configured physiological ceiling. F32 models a bounded species maturation increment; an adult receives no perpetual age bonus. Species curves and initial latent ranges are configured proposals, not recovered AO biology.

Worked interaction and exploit boundary

A character with core10 and learned skill80 uses effective skill10. Its simple F28 contest benchmark is floor(10/2)+10=15. Core80 with skill2 instead scores42: raw attributes can matter without granting the missing technique pattern. Neither benchmark bypasses hard acquisition, tool, affinity or permission gates. Mastery stages additionally require qualifying uses and distinct contexts (F62); a title, synergy bonus or borrowed tool cannot satisfy that evidence requirement.

02. Skill Synergy Matrix

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,4,6,8,11,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

A directed, scoped network

Synergy means transferable capability in a particular action, not universal mutual bonuses. The34 proposed skills retain distinct IDs and cores. Each registered directed edge specifies a source, target and required action tags. Only raw effective source levels from applicable edges enter F64/F05. The target's bonus is capped by its own effective level, and each source is counted once. The full34×34 matrix has12 scoped edges; an empty cell means no registered synergy, not an undiscovered secret bonus.

Resolution order

First derive each skill's effective level from learned XP, associated core and allowed temporary effects. Next choose the action's target skill and actual tags. Filter directed edges by those tags, canonicalize duplicate sources, then calculate the capped flat contribution. Finally add that contribution to the relevant contest score. Never feed a synergy-enhanced source back into another edge: the graph is not a recursively amplifying circuit. A source skill can help execution without becoming acquired knowledge of the target technique.

Concrete valid and invalid use

Two raw sources at20 and30 produce floor(20/10)+floor(30/10)=5 potential points. If the target is20 its F05 ceiling is4, so the result is4, provided both registered scopes match the actual action. This is a synthetic arithmetic explanation, not authorization to add two new edges. An unlisted relationship, wrong action tag, repeated source or chain of temporary synergy gains does not yield additional credit. Context is a typed input derived from the actual proposal, not a player declaring every advantageous tag.

Mastery remains separate. A novice may benefit from another trained capability, but cannot use the bonus to cross a learned-level or historical-use threshold. Improvements in a source do not retroactively rewrite earlier action results. Edge revisions need a new rule version and review, with old committed history preserved.

03. Affinity and Weakness System

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,4,5,6,7,9,11. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

What is and is not recovered

The finalized complete affinity roster, individual weaknesses, resistances and triangle directions are source-access UNKNOWN. This does not mean Aegis never created them. Water, Earth, Air, Lightning, Fire and Ice occur as explicit examples in the recovered archive, not as a finalized six-entry roster. Mud is an illustrative derived expression rather than evidence of an additional primary affinity. Generic ChatGPT assistant suggestions, including hypothetical triangle names, are not creator canon.

Distinct states

Ownership records that an affinity was acquired. Access records whether a specific actor can presently draw on it. Active drawing/attunement records which channels are engaged in this action. Knowledge, technique acquisition, technique mastery and permission are further distinct conditions. Owning multiple affinities neither reveals every technique nor enables simultaneous use beyond F09. Each accessible affinity has its own EA reserve (738f §9); AP remains separate.

The creator direction distinguishes an acquired affinity's weakness from defensive benefits gained through the affinity currently drawn upon. A future signed profile must therefore contain both the owned-affinity weakness facts and action-specific active defense rules. The current fixtures validate ownership/access/channel distinctions but do NOT implement a canonical weakness graph. Missing edges or immunity rules cannot be inferred from elemental intuition, names, symmetry or an assistant's examples.

Multi-affinity and progression handling

A technique's explicit profile identifies every required affinity and per-domain cost. All must be accessible and active within the channel limit. F71 counts distinct canonical acquired technique families whose actual profile contains a qualifying affinity. An alias cannot count twice. Under the proposed policy a multi-affinity family may count once toward each matching affinity's quota, but no mismatching family fills a quota. The policy is itself a proposal; unknown family maps or thresholds block Tier qualification.

No named affinity roster is manufactured to fill the requested catalogue. Once the exact roster and directed relationships are supplied, each record still needs provenance, effect scope, active-defense semantics, compatible technique mappings and negative tests before canonical acceptance can change.

04. Tier 9–0 Technique Catalogue

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,2,3,5,6,7,9,11,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Catalogue size and record contract

The requirement is ten distinct techniques, Tier9 through Tier0, for each established affinity:10×N. N remains UNKNOWN, so the verified canonical catalogue contains zero populated records, not a completed empty catalogue. The30-field schema linked below specifies purpose, application limits, prerequisites, attributes, skills, acquisition, costs, timing, spatial scope, effects, counters and mastery. Synthetic fixtures are executable examples of this schema's qualification layer, not final named AO techniques.

Knowing is not executing

The creator distinguishes learning about a Tier0 technique from actually executing it. The typed profile resolves canonical family IDs, required prior families, forbidden acquired families/incompatibilities, technique-specific mastery, affinity mastery, attribute/skill requirements and execution permission. Acquisition must be backed by knowledge. A complete explicit prerequisite graph is required; unresolved aliases, absent nodes and cyclic required-prior graphs reject. UNKNOWN maps do not become empty requirements.

The phase3 execute_technique entry derives qualification from a snapshot under the resource ledger lock; it accepts no arbitrary client qualified flag. It binds actor/profile/context, mode, object and time into the request payload before debiting resources. The older resource ledger remains a lower-level accounting primitive and is not a secure external qualification API. Its internal qualified=True is supplied only after typed derivation, not trusted from a player.

Tier and mode boundaries

There is no universal rule that an ordinary practitioner's Tier must equal a technique's Tier. Actual prerequisite conditions govern ordinary use. Azimah manifestations are a specific source restriction: existing body-Tier techniques at BASE only, normal costs and daily limits; no artificial AP or surge. Generic F11 enhanced/surge benchmarks are not evidence that every technique supports those modes. A profile must explicitly permit each mode and define its effect.

Failure, counters and mastery

Wrong affinity access, inactive required channel, missing prior acquisition, forbidden acquired family or insufficient specific mastery rejects before effect commitment. Preparation, activation and sustain are distinct phases; legitimately completed preparation is not refunded merely because a later phase fails. An alias or second stored object shares the same daily family counter. F63's learned execution stage depends on qualifying attempts, successes and contexts, not a narrative claim of mastery. Replay IDs bind the full payload, so changing a profile or payer under an old request cannot grant free execution.

Complete record fields

name, affinity, technique_tier, conceptual_purpose, description, valid_applications, invalid_applications, prerequisites, required_attributes, required_skills, attunement_requirements, acquisition_conditions, execution_conditions, resource_costs, activation_time, range_spatial_requirements, duration, area_targets, base_effect, enhanced_effect, triple_surge_effect, cooldown, daily_usage_limitations, failure_conditions, countermeasures, weakness_interactions, equipment_interactions, relevant_modifiers, possible_status_effects, mastery_progression.

See the typed schema and synthetic profile data. The fixture graph is not the canonical catalogue.

05. Resource Economy and Allocation

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,3,4,6,9,11,12,14,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Resource types, units and allocations

AP is the universal action resource; EA is separate and allocated to each accessible affinity. Do not invent a second generic mana or stamina reserve. Fatigue, injury and deprivation are conditions that influence permitted actions and recovery, not duplicate pools. F07 proposes AP maximum5×Constitution; F08 proposes per-affinity EA8×Attunement+4×min(Attunement, affinity control). Neither formula is an established canonical capacity. F09 limits simultaneously drawn domains independently of the number of owned pools.

The broad synthetic example with six pools at240EA has1440 totalEA but only one active channel atATT20. Recovery is a shared total budget allocated across domains, not a full independent regeneration stream for each owned affinity. A0.1EA/s total over60s returns6EA, not36. Replenishment caps at each maximum, uses explicit allocation shares and records its source; it is not an unaccounted transfer from an object or another actor.

Preparation, stored charge, activation and sustain

A stored reservoir preserves per-domain quantities, capacity, provenance and owner. F38's transfer efficiency makes100EA available produce80 stored and20 loss at efficiency4/5; charging cannot create charge. Activation can spend a reservoir's EA while still charging the actor's AP and daily family usage. A second object or renamed family does not create another daily allowance. Sustain stops at the exact payable time from F37; a reserve cannot go negative or fund an entire interval after it has emptied.

Preparation charges only the explicitly committed preparation phase. Execution validates derived technique gates, consent, geometry and funds before the activation result. AP recovery is disabled during active spend/sustain in the defined interval model to prevent a same-pool infinite loop. F36 facilities require a declared serviced capacity and active fuel; overcrowding limits benefit. Injury recovery factor F35 and nutrition constraints reduce actual replenishment, not merely its display.

World time, offline work and unknown reset policy

F34 needs an explicit positive world-day length and epoch. Complete a8fa says three world days per real day; complete738f says one AO day per three real days. Conflict C03 remains unresolved: no real-time conversion or guessed reset is selected. Azimah's shared-versus-body daily scope also remains UNKNOWN. Ordinary86400-second cycles used in work fixtures are named proposals, not a resolution of C03.

The combined OfflineSchedule reserves AP/material at task start, retains work-in-progress, consumes food/water over time and permits rest only when funded. Once nutrition fails, work and recovery stop while time/deprivation continue. Bulk and irregular step schedules are checked for identical state, rather than crediting a player for repeatedly reopening a simulation.

06. Tier Advancement Requirements

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,2,3,4,5,7,8,14,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Advancement is eligibility, not acquisition

Tier progresses from9 toward0; Tier2 is below Tier1. A lower numeral is not a universal linear damage multiplier. Proposed threshold rows below concern permanent specialist/support attributes, Attunement, learned skill, distinct affinities, technique families, mastery and conceptual evidence. Temporary equipment or modifiers never become permanent advancement credit. Passing a threshold check is not a committed Tier change, nor automatic acquisition of a technique or Heirloom.

Per-affinity prerequisite proof

An aggregate count alone is insufficient. evaluate_tier and advancement_eligible require an explicit family-to-affinity catalogue, qualifying affinities actually owned by the candidate, a positive integer quota for every qualifying affinity and a declared counting policy. F71 canonicalizes aliases and deduplicates acquisitions before filtering by each affinity's real association. Under the proposed per_affinity_unique_family policy the same multi-affinity family can contribute once to each matching quota; that convention is not canon. Unknown canonical quotas or incomplete graph data block rather than passing.

For a synthetic candidate requiring one A-associated family, acquiring only a B-associated family fails even if total acquired count is one. Acquiring a and its alias still gives one A family, not two. Mastery aliases must agree, and learned mastery is not inferred from an inflated effective contest score. The complete per-affinity map and quotas remain UNKNOWN for actual AO progression.

Evidence, Heirloom and transaction

Conceptual demonstrations come from unique committed evidence, with typed filters and actor attribution; rumors and replayed causal IDs do not supply extra demonstrations. The Heirloom authorization must identify this candidate and the relevant assigned Tier. Bounded attestation is a separately named fixture, not proof that the creator chose attestation instead of acquisition, consumption or custody.

F39 remains an older generic ritual QUOTE proposal, not the operative I06 profile or a learned technique/Heirloom-taking permission. The current AdvancementProfile explicitly supplies actual preparation/work/material/AP/per-domain EA/equipped-tool/custody/benefit policies. ProductionBook now executes these as full finite ritual transactions: reservation, preparation, timed work, failure/cancel, permanent Tier/benefit commitment and no-fill resource reconciliation. It is a local executable reference design, not a deployed production Kernel. Unknown canonical policies still block; completed preparation/work stays paid independently of an uncommitted Tier change.

Proposed threshold rows, not actual character sheets

Every row still requires explicit per-affinity technique quotas/maps (canonical values UNKNOWN), conceptual evidence and the appropriate authorized Heirloom policy. Aggregate family counts below never replace F71.

07. Conceptual Agent and Heirloom Register

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 3,4,5,6,9,10,12,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Canonical register versus synthetic authority profiles

No complete named Conceptual Agent roster, final conceptual identities or named Heirloom register has been verified in recovered sources. Kharad offices, generic simulation agents and NPC records must not be repurposed into Conceptual Agents. The executable fixture uses conspicuously synthetic IDs; its three operation kinds demonstrate a representation, not a newly declared AO pantheon.

Latest t99 assigns each Agent exactly one Heirloom at one particular Tier. It does not assign one Heirloom per Tier to every Agent. Earlier wording about choosing a next embodiment's Tier/type remains a documented tension, not authority for automatic epoch-based reassignment. The model preserves one persistent Heirloom identity and fixed assigned Tier; unsupported cross-Tier re-embodiment blocks. Creator authority and manifested embodiment remain different roles.

Identity, type and bounded reality authority

ConceptIdentity, AgentProfile, Embodiment and AuthorityRule separate the concept, the enduring Agent, the current physical body/type and each allowed operation. Resolution checks actual actor identity, embodiment state, fixed Tier, embodiment type, concept, target type, region, actual3D range, rule-specific target facts, physical Heirloom custody and permission. Membership in a generic set of operation names is not sufficient. An unknown fact/policy is not a favorable default.

CHANGE validates both current and proposed values against the rule's bounds and maximum delta through F74. TRIGGER can create only a registered pending-event kind. PREVENT requires that exact pending event and cannot erase committed history. Kernel, rule and identity override fields are reserved; a supplied extra parameter cannot transform a local target edit into global reality authority. F75 measures actual coordinate distance, including height, rather than trusting a target's asserted range or region label.

The fixture permits three invocations per Heirloom/rule at10m. These are synthetic finite limits, not canonical ranges, costs or recovery periods. Usage stays bound to the same Heirloom/rule across custody handoffs; gifting and reacquiring it does not reset the allowance. Broader authority requires an explicit profile, not rhetorical claims about conceptual power.

Relinquishment, death, pressure and return

Creator excerpts say giving away the Heirloom ends embodiment, and a slain Agent leaves its Heirloom. Fixtures retain an inert body or token placement under explicitly named proposed dispositions. They do not invent a canonical corpse policy. Later conceptual pressure must come from deduplicated committed matching causes, not self-awarded claims. Pressure alone cannot steal a token: re-embodiment additionally requires authorized physical return and the same fixed assigned Tier. There is no automatic recall, clone or custody reset.

Formula definitions and reproducible examples

F44, F59, F74, F75. Each linked entry supplies callable argument order, meanings, bounds and exactly two tested input/output vectors.

08. Modifier, Title, Blessing and Curse System

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,2,4,5,6,7,12,13,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Modifier identity and eligibility

A modifier is a versioned, attributable rule with a source, affected quantity, explicit scope, conditions, stacking group, duration and bounds. Titles, blessings, curses, gems, equipment, property and membership can supply different sources; their labels do not themselves grant a numerical bonus or permission. History predicates evaluate committed evidence with typed comparisons and explicit UNKNOWN handling. A rumor about an achievement is not proof that its grant conditions were met.

Eligibility for a title and possession of an authorized title grant are separate. Qualifying history is necessary where specified, but an eligible actor cannot self-issue an institutional or conceptual reward. The granting authority and permitted scope must be verified. Conversely, temporary suppression should not erase the historical fact that a title was legitimately granted. Revocation and renewed eligibility require their own recorded causes.

Numerical application and conflicts

F14 applies declared flat adjustments before multiplying nonnegative factors, then clamps to the affected domain's explicit bounds. Multiple effects must first pass source, scope, stacking and conflict checks; F14 is arithmetic, not a universal decision to stack everything. A negative flat adjustment can lower a score while a relative factor remains nonnegative. Injury or curse conditions can also prohibit an action outright, rather than being represented as an arbitrary negative attack number.

For example, the existing scenario starts at100, adds20 and subtracts30, then multiplies by3/2 and1/2, yielding135/2 before any tighter domain cap. That does not allow bypassing missing affinity access, required prior techniques, physical reach or Heirloom custody. Neither temporary attributes nor a current effective skill bonus satisfies a permanent Tier or learned-mastery predicate.

Evidence and anti-farming

F33 permits only approved typed fields from committed evidence and deduplicates by an explicitly chosen event/challenge/causal/context key. Replaying one rescue or renaming an identical challenge must not create five qualifying achievements. Ambiguous/unknown evidence blocks a grant rather than becoming an empty, trivially true predicate. Organizational membership conditions are evaluated at the relevant action time; expulsion can remove future benefits without deleting past grants or rewriting history.

Formula definitions and reproducible examples

F14, F33, F62, F65. Each linked entry supplies callable argument order, meanings, bounds and exactly two tested input/output vectors.

09. Object, Material, Tag and Crafting System

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,3,4,5,7,10,11,12,14,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Physical objects and materials

An object has persistent identity, dimensions, material mass, condition, owner, physical relation and provenance. Legal title is not position or possession. A sword owned by a merchant may physically lie on a corpse; neither fact automatically changes the other. Container interiors, apertures, closed state and payload limits are explicit. Parent relations cannot form cycles. Equipment contributes mass to the carrying subtree, and connector checks include upstream attachments rather than checking only the newest joint.

F40/F41 separate occupied material volume from a bounding envelope. A hollow box is not solid material filling every interior cubic meter. F42/F43 compare a rated connector load with actual mass and declared acceleration. F67 budgets sensible heating energy; a conductive tag cannot supply unlimited heat, and these operational fixtures are not complete thermodynamics or measured material engineering.

World anchoring and on/in relations

AnchoredWorld adds world/terrain/structure roots with bounded rectangular frames, an explicit ON plane or IN volume, and traceable ancestry for every object. Coordinates are not inventory slots and are not a full support/collision solver. Physical transfer requires a living actor, movement permission, actual reach to source and destination, and access through an open source container. A matching owner string or co-located coordinate does not prove an actor physically holds a corpse or Heirloom; physically_held follows the actual parent ancestry.

Envoy death, body-object conversion and equipment

PlayerLife requires an explicit player_envoy type. Committed death evidence binds the Sovereign, active body and time. A death transaction converts that same physical body to a corpse object, marks it nonliving, preserves equipment parent relations, ownership, location and total mass, and applies only an explicit life-debit policy. It does not spawn replacement gear or automatically respawn. The creator's three-life direction is preserved; the trailing “and one” remains ambiguous, not a fourth life.

A replacement binding may select only an already existing authorized body under an explicit timing/zero-life policy. Corpse reacquisition requires matching corpse provenance and actual possession by the active living body. Recovery is single-use and payload-bound; owning the corpse remotely, moving a foreign body or replaying a request cannot restore another life. F73 rejects overflow above a declared cap rather than silently clamping. The fixture's cost1, restore1, cap3, delay10s and retain-inert disposition are proposals; canonical values and corpse consumption remain UNKNOWN. Healing wounds is not life recovery, and no automatic NPC/Agent/Azimah enrollment is allowed.

Crafting and compatibility

F22 evaluates an explicitly configured binding profile and can declare incompatibility. Tags encode properties/constraints, not unrestricted concept authority. Crafting consumes actual materials, labor and capacity; originals, replicas, stored-charge objects and Heirlooms retain distinct identities. F44 requires each capability axis and authority operation of a replica to fit the original; a clever tag combination cannot exceed a restricted original's conceptual envelope.

Proposed operational materials

The12 profiles are authored fixtures, not measured material realism or a canonical substance list. Density and barrier stress refer only to their declared model roles; using an evocative material name does not import real-world or magical properties.

10. Rarity and Production System

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 4,7,9,12,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Rarity is not authority

The proposed ordinal rarity lottery is a production outcome model, not a declaration of canonical rarity names. F23 maps skill and quality to weights summing to100; item identity and a registered deterministic creation draw bind the result. Revealing the same item again cannot reroll it. Canceling an unwanted result must not restore all spent materials and effort for another independent attempt.

The F23 proposal now explicitly uses the same normalized integer expression in data, executable behavior, prose and examples. Without the parity term, the five weights sum to100−(b mod2), which is99 for odd b. The existing declared policy allocates that single integer remainder to ordinal0 while preserving all other weights. For b19, [41,25,19,13,1] is invalid at total99; [42,25,19,13,1] totals100. This algebra and the integer/nonnegative/cap constraints justify retaining the pre-existing code behavior, rather than treating code as correct by fiat. Skill, quality and draw bounds are validated; the distribution remains a PROPOSAL, not canonical AO rarity.

Originals, replicas and ceilings

A replica's rarity ceiling is independent of its allowed conceptual capability envelope. F44 compares energy, range, duration, targets, information depth and authority operation sets componentwise. Passing a scalar rarity check does not authorize a new operation. A same-Tier replica cannot improve a restricted original by optimizing tags, and a high-quality manufacture cannot copy a unique binding, personhood, sacrifice or legal ownership.

A ceiling redirects otherwise too-high rarity results under the explicit lottery policy; it does not create an original. Restricted items need a stable original ID, versioned blueprint channels, permitted envelope and reviewed copying rule. Disassembly/reassembly or batch renaming cannot manufacture a new unique identity or reset a production limit.

Production, failure and conservation

F24 chooses the limiting whole-unit quantity from available material, funded labor and capacity. With100 material units at4 per unit,1000 labor seconds at50 per unit, and capacity100, output is20 units;80 material and1000 labor are consumed,20 material remain. Lowering material to20 instead permits5 units. These are proposed example units, not an established AO recipe.

Mass production uses batch/item identities; limited runs preserve cumulative quantities; one-of-a-kind production preserves the creation event and capability signature. Failure should leave explicitly accounted scrap, loss or work-in-progress, never unrecorded deletion followed by free replacement. The model demonstrates bounded operational profiles, not complete statistical calibration for every recipe and social scarcity condition.

Formula definitions and reproducible examples

11. Combat and Physical Resolution

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,2,3,4,5,8,9,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Contact and physical feasibility precede chance

An action progresses through initiation, preparation, travel/detection, response, contact, defense, penetration, resistance/DR, injury and recovery. F16 is only a broad reach envelope. Exact fixture contact uses F45 translated axis-aligned boxes with relative motion; an in-range target is not automatically hit. Facing, height, intervening obstacles and swept target surfaces matter. F46 supports specific horizontal cones, not arbitrary posture or full rotational mesh physics.

Movement uses explicit comfortable load, terrain, profile speed and acceleration limits (F47–F49). A technique cannot roll through an obstacle or declare a longer arm. Actual weapon and target velocities determine relative contact speed; mismatched claimed speed or facing direction rejects. Detection and enough reaction time are necessary before any block calculation. F17/F51 additionally require readiness, consent, coverage and a legal response window; a high defense score does not invent an unseen response.

Energy, defense, material work and injury

F18 supplies kinetic energy from effective contact mass and relative speed. F51 removes only the authorized absorbed component up to an explicit block capacity. F50 models an operational armor work barrier from stress, contact area, thickness and condition, not physically complete penetration. F20 applies the defined resistance/DR ordering and treats True Damage as a separately qualified contact property. Being a nearby gas or describing an attack as “true” does not establish actual contact or the effect property.

Coverage is local: a helmet does not protect a foot. F21 reduces DR with condition; F69 records work-to-condition wear. F52 bounds regional injury severity. F68 compares a conservative impulse fixture with restoring capacity to model a posture change; it is not a complete balance/support solver. A2kg effective weapon at10m/s has100J, while4kg at5m/s has50J: larger mass alone does not guarantee greater damage.

Ongoing wounds and recovery

F66 gives a configured bleeding rate from a wound already established by contact. F53 decreases severity under Constitution, nutrition and elapsed rest, but preserves a missing-part condition; it does not grow a severed limb back or restore a player's life. F35 and F70 connect injury and deprivation to replenishment/ordinary action capability. Treatments require real access, material and permission, with committed results rather than narrative erasure of a wound event.

The thirteen-stage action framework rejects impossible geometry before any successful probability result can commit. Idempotent event payloads prevent reusing the same impact ID with larger energy. Existing tests cover specific translated-box, armor, posture, wear and recovery cases; they do not prove arbitrary fluid/contact physics, anatomical death thresholds or calibrated medical realism.

12. Property and Organizational Modifier System

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 5,8,9,10,13,14,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Property, institutions and permissions

Property is a legal relationship backed by grants and transactions, not merely an object's physical parent. Possession, title, location, institutional membership, reputation and public knowledge are independently recorded. A holder may lack legal ownership; a legal owner may lack physical access. Joining an organization does not grant every asset, disclose every conversation or make a conceptual technique equivalent to institutional authority.

F65 proposes explicit institution stages: valid charter and sufficient members permit formation; actual assets, stock and treasury permit operation; a jurisdictional attestation is additionally needed for recognition. These thresholds are fixture proposals, not universal AO law. Development and privileges must name a granting authority, scope, conditions and expiry/revocation. Expulsion removes applicable future benefits; it does not erase history or silently confiscate every privately owned item.

Conserving economic transactions

Income, upkeep, treasury and debt use abstract in-world currency subunits, never a real payment rail. F25 may produce a signed balance for accounting; it is not permission to spend nonexistent funds. Transfers reconcile counterparties, escrow or debt explicitly. F54 aggregates taxable income over the defined period before applying brackets so splitting one payment into many small transfers cannot evade the same total tax. F55 calculates only an explicitly agreed simple rate over an explicit period, not an unstated compounding or collection authority.

A production or trade success is bounded by actual stock, transport and demand through F57. Ownership transfer does not duplicate an asset across organization and household books. Work-in-progress, reserves and scrap need one accountable location; online/offline realization cannot count them twice. A facility's replenishment bonus must be serviced and fueled, not merely claimed by a building title.

Law, belief and reputation

A witnessed allegation is a belief record with provenance, not omniscient legal truth. A judgment requires the defined jurisdiction/evidence/permission contract, while information reaches participants through actual routes and delay. Reputation can influence future probabilities or eligibility without mechanically coercing every NPC or changing a past event. National, city, organizational and household scopes remain distinct; benefits need explicit stacking conditions rather than being multiplied by every affiliation automatically.

13. World Influence Integration

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 8,12,14,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Influence is a causal bias, not guaranteed reality

World Influence distinguishes individual, household, organization, settlement, city, nation, region and world scopes. Gravity is one family of outcomes concerning attraction, repulsion, reach and retention; it is not permission to reinterpret every influence value as literal Newtonian force or make every desired event inevitable. Contributions need committed causes, scope and provenance rather than a self-declared importance score.

The implementation separates opportunity frequency, probability of an opportunity succeeding, quantity actually transferable, information delay and pressure retention. F56 accumulates fractional opportunity carry over time; changing time step cannot create extra trials. F26 changes a defined base probability within its bounds, not quantity or guaranteed arrival. F57 caps a successful event by stock, transport, demand and available population. These independent stages prevent one favorable influence number from multiplying every axis of the event.

Propagation and retained causes

F58 requires distance, route speed and relay delay. A distant NPC cannot know a rumor before it arrives, even if the underlying Kernel records its source immediately. F59 decays prior pressure and adds unique causal contributions, with finite bounds. Shared household/organization membership alone does not broadcast private knowledge or allow the same cause to be counted at every scope as fresh pressure.

A successful migrant or trade opportunity transfers existing people or stock. It must debit the origin and credit the destination without duplicating population, wealth or supplies. The local fixture separates event frequency from success quantity; it is not a calibrated demographic or market model. A blocked route, absent stock or zero legal demand can keep the actual outcome at zero despite favorable probability.

Offline activity and anti-loop policy

NPC work, law, information and trade continue under time-accounted schedules when Sovereigns are offline. Logging in or re-querying an event cannot reset its seed, create new time or redraw a failed opportunity. Stable event/cause IDs bind the opportunity and evidence. Coarse and realized population states must share the same reserves, not keep two copies of the source resources.

14. NPC Mechanics Integration

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1,2,3,4,5,8,9,11,12,13,15. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Statistical, weighted and realized inhabitants

An NPC is subject to the same ordinary action prerequisites, physical constraints, resources and knowledge limits as an Envoy unless a separately sourced exception applies. Population representation changes computational detail, not the existence of people or assets. F27 conserves statistical, weighted and realized counts. Weighting transfers one existing inhabitant and an actual allocation of stock; realization exposes a previously frozen identity/profile rather than sampling a more favorable person each time.

F60 freezes a configured latent attribute draw at identity creation. Re-realizing the same NPC retains its attributes, goods and history. Coarsening does not erase debt, membership or injuries, and death does not become resurrection on the next load. An NPC estate may remain as assets even while living population decreases; those assets cannot be reissued to both the cohort and a replacement inhabitant.

Learning and long-horizon resources

NPC growth uses meaningful training, recovery, external XP and bounded maturation, not a free level for each simulation tick. Learned techniques and mastery need the same typed prerequisite graph and evidence as other ordinary actors. Social plans use available information and can rationally fail when beliefs are wrong. Neither the assistant's archive access nor Kernel omniscience is an NPC sense.

The proposed ordinary work cycle has86400 world seconds, an eight-hour work window and sixteen-hour rest. Each300-second job reserves1AP and4 material for1 finished good; daily needs are1 food and3 water. The combined OfflineSchedule keeps incomplete work as work-in-progress and consumes needs throughout elapsed time. At starvation, new work and AP recovery stop while deprivation continues. Thirty funded cycles produce2500 goods from10000 material in the tested fixture; that is an operational accounting example, not canonical NPC productivity.

Player lives are not general NPC rules

The recovered three-life statement concerns players/Sovereign–Envoy bodies. LifeBook requires explicit player_envoy typing and otherwise blocks. It is not applied automatically to NPCs, Conceptual Agents or Azimah. Agent slain/Heirloom lifecycle and ordinary population death are separate subsystems. A newly spawned NPC is not a replacement Envoy unless a future sourced, authorized binding policy establishes that identity and transaction. Healing a living NPC or retaining a corpse never by itself restores a Sovereign life.

15. Kernel Resolution Rules

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1–14,16,18,19. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Underlying authority and manifested actors

Latest t99/t100 identifies Azimah as Original Creator/Kernel while separating bounded embodied interventions and narration. Preserve the older60c3 statement that she does not replace the deterministic Kernel as a source conflict, not a reason to merge roles. The local TraceEngine is only a reference implementation of a deterministic contract, not the production Kernel or a live authority grant.

The underlying authority validates and commits authorized changes; a manifestation submits a proposal like any constrained actor. Narration describes committed results and does not supply missing effects, permission, resources or hidden information. The Original Creator/Kernel has no ordinary finite AP/EA sheet. Bodies have real constraints and cannot inherit direct commit because of their identity's narrative significance.

Thirteen-stage action contract

  1. Intent records an actor's request, not trusted outcome text.
  2. A structured proposal binds action/rule versions, actor, targets, arguments, expected state version and immutable request ID.
  3. Prerequisites derive acquisition, technique graph, mastery, body/tool and affinity conditions.
  4. Resource validation checks available AP, per-domain EA, objects, daily limits and committed preparation.
  5. Permission validation applies operation-specific authority, consent and scope.
  6. Spatial/physical validation establishes actual reach, containment, contact or anchored custody.
  7. Applicable modifiers are filtered before arithmetic; none repairs a failed hard gate.
  8. Deterministic resolution computes the registered rule on exact inputs.
  9. The provisional outcome describes proposed deltas without publishing them as reality.
  10. Reconciliation checks expected versions and mechanic-specific invariants/conservation.
  11. Commitment changes authoritative local state once.
  12. History appends the causal record and hash-chain link.
  13. Projection reveals only authorized results, not every internal state field.

Transaction identity and exploit defense

A repeated request with the same complete payload returns its recorded result; the same ID with altered payload rejects. Concurrent claims on a last use or one corpse recovery serialize so only one can commit. Reconciliation recomputes the expected transition; a forged nonnegative life increase is still rejected rather than accepted by a simple bounds check. Stable aliases and Heirloom IDs prevent identity-reset counters.

Life transactions require committed death evidence, matching Sovereign/body/corpse provenance, explicit policy and actual physical custody. Agent authority requires typed operation-specific preconditions and scope at resolution. These cannot be replaced by a generic client permission set or title. UNKNOWN remains a blocked policy/input, not an instruction for natural-language improvisation.

The model uses in-process locks and hash chains. It does not claim durable atomic commits across databases, authenticated external callers, distributed consensus or rollback recovery after process crashes. Those production requirements remain outside this design implementation.

16. Numerical Balance and Exploit Test Report

Overall status: IN PROGRESS. Current source-independent working proposal; not canon or production Kernel. Numerical proposals and synthetic fixtures cannot establish unknown source records.

Assumptions and dependencies

Dependencies: 1–15,17,18,19. Exact arithmetic uses world time and declared units. Required UNKNOWN facts/policies block; do not silently substitute defaults. Latest t99 working direction and t100 role/body restrictions prevail while older conflicting records remain attributed.

Evidence and what a PASS means

The complete CURRENT-tree suite is executed during this consistency release, with exact timestamp, passed/failed/error/skipped counts and per-test records in tests/summary.json. The extracted package reruns that same full suite; its separate result is recorded in documentation-artifact-verification.json. Historical406-pass evidence is retained but is not reused as the current count. The100 formulas now have200 fixed example checks; additional odd-parity, current-prose and snapshot regressions are included. A PASS is local proposal evidence, not canonical balance acceptance.

There are18 original major-mechanic legal/invalid trace pairs plus11 prior profile pairs plus18 I01/I02 lifecycle pairs, each using the actual thirteen-stage transition framework. The three-role matrix has170 subjects and510 role records, plus156 executed subsystem boundary records. Matrix presence is not equivalent to running every possible interaction or validating exact Azimah statistics. In particular, unknown sheets remain UNKNOWN even when a synthetic vector passes.

Important fixed-vector and exploit coverage

Learning tests check low core/high learned skill, high raw attribute with little training, repetition recovery, sequential growth cost and mastery evidence. Resource tests check broad versus focused affinity pools, shared total recovery, stored charge loss, daily alias limits, last-use contention and payable depletion. Object/combat tests check closed containers, ancestors' connector loads, coverage, actual relative speed, readiness and replayed impacts.

Progression tests now reject aggregate counts supplied without the actual per-affinity map/quotas, and reject borrowed temporary statistics. Technique tests derive prior/forbidden acquisition and specific mastery instead of accepting a boolean. Player-life tests preserve equipment/mass/provenance, reject remote-title/foreign-corpse recovery and serialize simultaneous restoration. Agent tests enforce type/concept/facts/region/range, pending-versus-committed events, fixed assignment, voluntary return and counter continuity.

Documentation-specific verification

This pass separately validates all100 formula IDs, exactly two copied numerical examples per formula, callable argument order and all200 executable results against current declared JSON and functions. Markdown sections, local links, UTF-8 encoding, source-role labels and HTML/archive payloads are checked independently. No statistical claim is inferred from those structural checks.

The checkpoint-specific preservation assertion now verifies every one of the24 original files INSIDE its exact checksum snapshot, including CRC, original file names, sizes and hashes. New current-content and unchanged-source/historical-archive assertions independently verify the authorized present tree. The historical test/version and just-delivered bundle/HTML are retained in the pre-correction snapshot. No behavioral test was removed, weakened or skipped; authorized new documents are no longer falsely required to equal the old prose.

17. Unresolved Decisions Register

18. Final Cross-System Consistency Report

Current consistency result

The formula reference reproduces100 formulas and200 exact test vectors. This is a coherent working proposal checkpoint, not completion of the full t99/t100 acceptance requirement.

The cross-system invariants are: independent permanent development; learned knowledge distinct from execution; per-domain EA distinct from AP/channel capacity; aliases and duplicate evidence cannot multiply benefit; possession distinct from legal ownership; resources, equipment and population conserve across representation changes; committed history is not an editable belief; and manifested interventions cannot acquire Kernel commitment authority. Later typed profiles strengthen older accounting primitives rather than treating them as sufficient external authorization APIs.

Corrected phase3 interactions

Tier eligibility checks actual acquired technique families associated with every qualifying affinity, not just a total count. Technique qualification derives the prior/forbidden/mastery/access conditions. Player death preserves the same body and equipment; only explicitly typed player policy can debit/restore lives through real corpse transactions. ON/IN anchors support physical provenance without pretending to solve all support physics. Agents exercise typed trigger/prevent/change authority only within their actual embodiment, facts, custody and spatial limits; one Heirloom keeps its assigned Tier.

These constraints apply together. A powerful title cannot supply a missing technique, a stored reservoir cannot reset a daily allowance, a legal ownership certificate cannot count as holding a corpse, and re-embodiment cannot reset the Heirloom's invocation counter or rewrite its Tier. A successful fixture demonstrates these finite rules, not the entire canonical setting.

Creator/Kernel, Tier1 and Tier2 acceptance

The Original Creator/Kernel is underlying authority without an ordinary finite sheet. Tier1 and Tier2 are bounded forms; Tier2 is below Tier1 and neither inherits direct commit, shared thoughts/locations, unlimited group teleport or artificial AP. Their exact established values are not recovered and cannot be replaced by the proposed threshold table. Supporting19 separates applicable rules, capabilities, limits, calculations, valid/invalid examples, contradictions and corrections for these roles.

All18 deliverables remain IN PROGRESS for overall acceptance. 19. Mandatory Azimah cross-system validation

Overall status: IN PROGRESS — exact body acceptance BLOCKED source_access. This document separates the underlying Original Creator/Kernel from manifested Tier1 and Tier2. It does not manufacture reference statistics to obtain a PASS. Source: latest t99/t100; older contradictory Azimah wording remains attributed in C02.

Reference A: Original Creator / Kernel

Applicable rules are authorization, state validation, deterministic resolution, reconciliation, commitment, persistence/history and permitted projection. Capability is underlying authority over registered reality rules, not an ordinary character technique list. Limitation is the distinction between authorized internal state and what a manifestation, narrator or NPC can perceive or publish. Finite AP/EA, ordinary core attributes and character death/lives are not numerical Kernel properties.

Valid example: the local registered resolver derives a permitted bounded change, reconciles the exact candidate state and appends its history once. Invalid: narration or a manifested body writes a committed result directly, grants itself a permission or exposes another body's secret location. The local engine demonstrates structural behavior; it is not production Kernel deployment. C02 is corrected by latest creator precedence while preserving the old source, not by assigning the Kernel a larger character sheet.

Reference B: Tier1 body

Only existing body-Tier BASE techniques are available under the explicit manifestation constraint, with ordinary actual prerequisites, costs and daily limits. Knowing a technique is not acquiring it; acquisition does not waive access, active channels, prior/forbidden techniques or specific mastery. The actor must have actual equipment, location, permissions and resources. Its exact attributes, skills, affinities, techniques, EA/AP reserves, mastery and Heirloom are UNKNOWN in recovered source material.

A synthetic T1 profile can illustrate base-cost arithmetic, but cannot stand in for the actual body. Valid conditional example: with sourced acquisition, matching affinity access and sufficient actual reserve, it submits a physically legal base-mode proposal. Invalid: the same body selects enhanced/surge, obtains artificial AP, commits its desired outcome directly or claims unlimited range by invoking Creator identity. Corrective action is to source its actual sheet/profile; do not backfill it from the proposed Tier threshold table.

Reference C: Tier2 body

Tier2 is below Tier1 in9→0 ordering, not an upgrade. This form has its own UNKNOWN established state and no automatic inheritance of Tier1 mastery, permission or resources. It follows the same underlying/manifested boundary, with its own existing body-Tier BASE techniques. Exact differences must be read from sources rather than invented from the number.

Valid conditional example: a sourced Tier2 technique meeting its own typed prerequisites submits an ordinary funded proposal. Invalid: treat Tier2 as higher than Tier1, execute a Tier1-only pattern without its sourced conditions or borrow the other body's senses/location. The generic proposed F11 benchmark would distinguish T1 cost2048EA from T2 cost1024EA at mode1; those are arithmetic of a generic proposal, NOT either body's actual technique cost or available reserve. No simultaneous existence of the two forms is assumed.

Shared embodied constraints and their boundaries

The creator allows at most nine potential manifested bodies, with independent senses, no shared thoughts or locations, and only the sourced split-moment/region awareness. This does not grant a global radar or a pool that multiplies with body count. Teleport is one person or a sourced small group; the exact group bound is UNKNOWN and cannot be inferred as nine. Artificial AP/supercharge and body direct commitment are forbidden. Global-versus-body daily-counter attribution remains UNKNOWN, so source-bound execution blocks instead of selecting a favorable counter scope.

Player life rules are NOT APPLICABLE to Azimah by default. F72/F73 demonstrate explicitly typed ordinary player-envoy policies only; do not give these bodies three lives or invent a Kernel death counter. Agent embodiment and Heirloom lifecycle are also distinct from Azimah body rules unless exact sources explicitly connect them.

Argument order is the inspected executable signature, not an inferred variable order. Strings such as "3/2" represent exact rational values through the verifier's recursive conversion; null means no result/contact where the function defines it. None of these numbers are canon.

Global scope and authority

Hard prerequisites, type/permission/identity checks and conservation are not waived by a favorable numeric result. Ordinary actors have no universal practitioner-Tier equals technique-Tier gate; manifestations have their explicitly sourced body-Tier BASE restriction. Kernel authority is not a character pool. Exact Tier1/Tier2 input sheets remain UNKNOWN. F72/F73 player-life arithmetic is NOT APPLICABLE to Azimah by default.

F01 — Effective attribute

Status: PROPOSAL.

Equation as recorded in the current dataset:

max(1, base + permanent + temporary)

Executable and argument order: reference_math.effective_attribute(base, permanent=0, temporary=0).

Variable meanings, units and output: base/permanent/temporary: score points; output current effective score

Bounds and missing/invalid inputs: Permanent base used for advancement; equipment/current score only execution. Base integer>=1. Signed adjustments allowed. Missing required input: reject with RULE_INPUT_MISSING; do not guess.

Exploit defense and separate gates: Suppress negative scores at1; never turn temporary bonuses into base growth.

Variable meanings, units and output: damage is nonnegative injury units. ownedWeakness lists dimensionless factors≥1 for every owned affinity; activeRetained lists factors in (0,1] for only currently active accessible channels. minimumRetained∈(0,1] is dimensionless; output injury units. Empty products equal1.

Bounds and missing/invalid inputs: Incomplete registered maps or UNKNOWN rules block. Nonpositive active factors, factors above1, weakness below1, negative damage and invalid minimum reject. No guessed neutral absent edge.

Exploit defense and separate gates: Positive retained floor prevents free immunity; all owned weaknesses persist regardless of draw, channel and access checks precede arithmetic. This multiplier policy is a proposal, not a recovered triangle.

Exploit defense and separate gates: Reserve every AP/EA unit and every limited-run slot atomically; actual consumed inputs must cover condition-limited usable mass. No free quantity/time or partial-output cancellation.

each share = floor(total×w/sum w); leftover units go to largest remainders, then stable identity order

Executable and argument order: development_mechanics.integer_allocation(total, weights).

Variable meanings, units and output: total is a nonnegative integer XP/credit budget; weights are positive integers keyed by skill or attribute identity. Output shares are integers that sum exactly to total.

Bounds and missing/invalid inputs: total outside0–10000, empty/non-dict weights, non-ident keys or weights outside1–1000 reject.

Exploit defense and separate gates: No second profile may allocate the same causal event; leftover assignment is deterministic and cannot be shopped by renaming after the fact.

Two-vector bounds (I10): Each formula has two executed input/output vectors plus explicit rejection bounds; they are synthetic proposal numbers, not canonical coefficients.

Typed lifecycle and actual accounting

LifecycleBook coordinates existing derived technique qualification, ResourceBook, AnchoredWorld/ObjectGraph and AuthorityBook. It snapshots and rolls back all participating local state under its/resource/authority locks, verifies total physical mass, capacity including escrow, and exact per-job/per-compartment escrow reconciliation. This is single-process serializability through this coordinator, not distributed storage/CAS or protection from trusted code directly modifying graph internals. Operation IDs bind complete payloads; identical replay returns the original event, conflicting replay rejects. One reserved action per actor, distinct reserved physical rounds, locked consumed material identities and Agent slots prevent concurrent duplicate spending/creation.

Start derives acquisition, prior/forbidden families, affinity access and actual active channels, technique-specific mastery, attributes/skills, supported mode and permission. It never accepts a client qualified boolean. There is no universal ordinary practitioner-Tier equals technique-Tier gate. Manifestations alone retain body-Tier BASE. Unspecified phase/cost/durability/target facts block rather than filling defaults.

The explicit synthetic shot has2s preparation/2AP,1s connection/1AP+1EA(A),1s activation/2AP+8EA(A), then up to4s sustain at2EA(A)/s and10s absolute cooldown from activation. It reserves5AP and17EA, immediately spends preparation, and reserves one actual0.1kg round. Successful expiry leaves95AP/83EA from100/100. Failure of actual range connection retains only3AP/1EA, refunds unspent activation/sustain and releases the unused round; no daily activation is consumed. Cancellation2s into sustain leaves95AP/87EA. Those are explicit fixture values, not recovered technique records.

F76 computes available balance minus reservations; reserved quantities are removed from spendable pools but still occupy maximum capacity. Recovery/charging cannot fill escrow's capacity and overflow a later refund. The optional held charged object pays its own connection/activation/sustain EA; the caster pays AP. Actor EA is not quietly substituted. AP, each affinity EA, channel capacity, finite round identities, durability and family daily counters remain different quantities.

F77 uses absolute deadlines. Activation rechecks actual target range, permission, acquisition/access/channels, round custody, payer condition and funded tool durability. F69 wear is applied exactly once to the actual registered held tool; the synthetic durability policy requires remaining positive condition. Break-through activation outcomes are canonical UNKNOWN. Fixture zero wear is explicitly declared, never an implicit canonical zero. The registered target connection domain is actual3D distance plus trusted typed target permission, not full collision/occlusion/line-of-sight physics. I03 retains detailed combat scheduling/paths.

Daily buckets use an explicit synthetic86400-world-second day and actor-identity policy; the archive's contradictory AO/realtime ratios are not resolved here. Cooldown remains absolute across the bucket boundary. Both admission and activation check limits; preparation is not a reservation that guarantees later entitlement. Actual manifestation daily attribution stays UNKNOWN and blocks canonical execution. Sustain charges each domain for the same exact elapsed interval, caps at expiry and cannot reverse time. Expiry is resolved when the authoritative schedule advances; this is not a wall-clock background process. The shared ResourceBook rejects simultaneous rest during active sustain/work; recovery cannot advance its clock beneath a reserved continuous interval.

Rounds move from actual held→reserved→spent/recoverable at a target world anchor. A separate funded, physically reachable recovery moves that SAME object back. This fixture permits one recovery per round identity over its lifetime; it does not manufacture infinite reloads. Canonical ammunition survival/recovery policy is UNKNOWN. Range, custody or state failures roll back before any new debit. Ownership is retained when a round or tool moves; possession is not legal title.

Finite ordinary actions and defense

F81 quotes positive AP/time. The fixture walks one horizontal integer axis at2AP and1s per meter;2m costs4AP/2s. Handling a real permitted nearby object costs1AP/1s and changes physical possession, not ownership. Recovery costs1AP/1s; Heirloom breaking costs3AP/1s plus actual destruction permission and custody. Reserved work excludes ordinary actions by that actor. This is bounded spatial anchoring, not an inventory-slot system, obstacle/path solver, or full support/contact physics.

The authoritative registered defense profile separately records owned affinity weaknesses and active draw benefits. F78 multiplies every owned weakness (including inactive owned affinities), then only actual active/access-legal channel retention factors, with a strictly positive floor. At100 injury, owned weakness1.5 and two active0.5 factors yield37.5; one active factor yields75. Each explicit fixture defense also pays1AP/1s and1EA per actually active domain: two domains cost1AP+2EA, one costs1AP+1EA. UNKNOWN fees block. Defense during reserved work blocks pending I03 scheduling, rather than assuming a free simultaneous reaction. Two0.1 factors with a0.1 floor yield10, not immunity. Missing maps/edges block; no named triangle or neutral missing edge is guessed. The result is an exact proposed injury operand, not a complete technique-specific wound/effect library.

Three Heirloom-family transactions

All three require explicit creation/disposition profiles, actual owned unconsumed nonliving leaf materials, sufficient funded work, permissions and unique output identity. The fixture reserves2AP preparation plus4s at1AP/s;6kg input becomes4kg output+2kg scrap. Input identities become consumed zero-mass tombstones with provenance, while a real held workpiece carries their mass. F79 partitions actual output/scrap; F80 charges actual work. Failed completed work yields6kg scrap, no output, and6AP spent. Cancellation at2s retains4AP of paid work/preparation and6kg scrap. Unspent AP refunds do not restore material identities or techniques. There is no instant free craft.

Sacrificial family: committing prepared work irreversibly canonicalizes and removes the acquired sacrificed technique family, including aliases/variants, and records permanent lockout. Knowing the pattern may remain; reacquisition/relearning does not restore availability. Even manually corrupted reacquisition fails derived actor validation. Failure, interruption or breaking the resulting physical object never restores that family. No power or mastery is automatically granted to the item; any usable effect needs a separately qualified explicit authority/effect profile. Canonical transfer/embedded-use rules remain UNKNOWN.

Conscious family: successful work creates a persistent new conscious identity bound to object and creation event. Actual permitted funded breaking ends that identity and creates conserving fragments. Repair of the same original entity rejects. Explicit synthetic reassembly consumes the fragments into a newly created identity with ancestor provenance; the old identity remains ended. This is not healing, resurrection, a duplicated person, or a claim that canonical consciousness authority/capabilities were recovered.

Conceptual Agent family: an explicit synthetic registered creation policy must supply typed conceptual identity, embodiment type, bounded trigger/prevent/change rules and one fixed assigned Tier. Actual creation reserves an Agent slot and yields one physical token linked to the existing AuthorityBook; duplicate identity/token/output/concurrent attempt rejects. Latest t99 single assignment prevails. Being a Creator manifestation does not grant creation permission. Unknown real creation/destruction/custody policy blocks. An optional explicitly named protected-singleton fixture denies token destruction; body slaying is separate and leaves the actual Heirloom/equipment on the inert body. A permitted nearby gift ends embodiment and transfers only that actual token under the existing fixture policy. Existing pressure/physical voluntary-return/embodiment gates remain; no automatic epoch reassignment or involuntary recall was added.

For all families, rarity remains a separately governed property: crafting does not automatically promote it or unlock a conceptual effect. Canonical rarity/authority/history records are UNKNOWN, not uncreated. Provenance stores inputs, work, event history, sacrifice/conscious/Agent identity and fixed assignment as appropriate. General quality/tag/grade/recipe effects and original/replica promotion remain I05, not silently invented here.

Authority, clock and finite geometry

TemporalBook extends the same LifecycleBook coordinator and resource/object/authority books; it does not create a parallel unlimited AP pool. Existing derived knowledge/acquisition/prior/forbidden/mastery/access/channel/permission checks remain. The execution-actor hook computes actual temporary attribute/skill operands without rewriting permanent learned state; ordinary actors still have no universal practitioner-Tier equals technique-Tier gate. Manifestations retain body-Tier BASE only.

The authoritative clock advances integer world seconds, at most1000 per call; reaction register≤256 and depth≤2. Profiles are explicit synthetic records. Initiative uses existing F28 from actual effective Dexterity, capped effective reaction skill and declared profile bias. F82 orders time, stage, descending initiative, stable action ID. Equal-time ties do not depend on arrival order or thread scheduling; incompatible reservations by one actor cannot both be admitted. External cancellation is an explicit current-time choice; queued consent revocation precedes activation at that tick.

Geometry is exact axis-aligned boxes in a finite declared local test region; its100m coordinate frame is not the planet or focus continent. Obstacles use actual object positions/dimensions. Sensing checks actual projectile position, range, facing and swept line obstruction. Evasion/ordinary axis movement pays its setup/work duration and performs a swept final one-second displacement≤6m; it is NOT continuous locomotion for that entire setup period. Both the moving target and projectile paths enter existing relative swept contact. Contact requiring an irrational relative speed is outside this exact-rational domain and rejects, rather than receiving a fabricated speed. No arbitrary deformable bodies, diagonal freeform paths, unregistered obstacles or full support physics are claimed.

The scheduled-projectile policy leaves the same physical reserved round at the launch anchor after real typed activation. A flight must reference that actual committed job/round, may launch once, uses actual round mass and a declared finite axis velocity/lifetime. Contact is derived from swept boxes, with obstacles winning exact geometric ties. Physical contact time and stopped round position are retained; no title-only remote hit or guessed contact. A paid ward must be ready by exact contact time, actually equipped/held, permitted and still qualified, with its paid active channels unchanged. Readiness cannot be backdated from the logical resolution tick.

Six distinct reaction workflows

Detect: a chosen, consented, funded sensing action. Successful resolution commits observer-specific evidence with actual attack identity, time and expiry. Missing/late/expired detection cannot be replaced by a client flag. Failure of facing/range/obstruction retains paid preparation but refunds unspent activation. Detect is not Evade and does not prove sufficient time/space for one.

Evade: requires fresh actual detection, actual threatened body, standing posture, consent and a clear bounded path. It reserves finite AP, commits preparation immediately, then advances actual work under current slow/prohibition state. At completion it revalidates path/permission/target/detection/consent and spends activation before a physical displacement. If the projectile resolves first, the pending evade cancels without refunding preparation. It never teleports because a probability roll succeeded.

Counter: a chosen response using an acquired, qualified explicit local counter-block technique. Normal preparation/connection/activation, EA, AP, cooldown and daily family usage pass through LifecycleBook. The whole known defense budget is also reserved, including its additional AP/per-active-domain EA; a nearly empty actor cannot arm a counter known to be unfundable. The fixture creates a one-attack physical shield ward after paid defense time. Existing swept contact/F51 blocking/armor/injury/posture/F69 wear resolve the result. This is one supported local counter-block, not a universal counterattack or automatic erasure of any incoming effect.

Readied: pays preparation and reserves an ordinary response while waiting. A qualifying opportunity is not permission to spend activation: the owner must make an explicit choose after actual detection. No choice means no movement/activation; expiry or resolved threat releases only unspent reservations.

Prepared: actual technique preparation/connection may finish before the opportunity. Activation remains reserved until the owner makes an explicit detected/consented choice. Failed connection, later lost qualification or cancellation preserves already committed preparation/connection costs, never grants free activation or daily-counter bypass.

Triggered: preauthorizes a narrowly bound actual event/attack with an explicit revocable consent token. It may wait for real detection, then autonomously schedule the declared activation, without a new manual choice. No real trigger means no automatic spend. Revocation, expiry, target changes, lost prerequisites or interruption invalidate activation. One reaction family per actor/causal threat and bounded depth prevent counter/trigger recursion loops; no automatic unbounded chain of attacks is generated.

Only funded Detect may overlap an already waiting Readied/Prepared/Triggered reservation. Escrow is reconciled across both jobs, and shared recovery remains blocked until all reservations end. This is the precise newly enabled concurrency path, not general free simultaneous work/defense. The original I01 block remains for unregistered paths.

Resource, physical and timing examples

A blocked path is rejected before reservation. A cancelled Readied response leaves96AP after Detect: preparation remains spent,8 unspent activation AP returns.

The ordinary counter technique costs1 preparation AP,2 activation AP and8EA(A); its separate physical defense costs1AP+1EA(A)/1s. Including Detect, a successful ordinary fixture ends93AP/91EA from100/100. A prepared counter cancelled before activation keeps only its1 preparation AP spent; held activation/ward resources return. The very same actor/alias daily family counter and absolute cooldown apply. Real manifestation daily scope remains UNKNOWN; only explicitly labeled synthetic actor-identity scopes execute.
