Timed status application, resistance and treatment

Four labeled synthetic profiles implement burning, poison, paralysis and slow. Every application identifies actor/source, target, actual finite reagent/dose, explicit profile, finite resistance budget, AP/time, duration and provenance. UNKNOWN dose/profile/cost/resistance blocks; no missing entry becomes immunity or neutral zero. One explicit unit has a finite declared kg/unit. The consumed compound becomes actual spent/delivered matter with unchanged legal ownership, preserving mass. Application completes only with actual remaining access/permission; failure does not restore used compound or paid work. Contact-backed attempts bind an actual committed contact cause and deduplicate across request aliases.

F84 consumes a finite resistance budget: dose5 against budget2 yields effective3/budget0; dose1 against budget3 yields effective0/budget2. Complete absorption is a known consumed resource result, not infinite innate immunity. These fixture quantities are not realistic toxicology or an elemental weakness graph.

Burning/poison integrate F85 over each exact overlap of[start,end). Dose2 at2 severity-points/(dose-unit·s), starting1/ending5, contributes16 points over four seconds and zero after5. Burning's finite consumed dose/fuel and duration bound its proposed injury budget; calibrated heat/chemical physics is not claimed. Multiple actual sources stack; the same causal attempt cannot create duplicate effects. Paralysis prohibits this fixture's whole-body actions until exact expiry. Slow dose1/coefficient1 gives time factor2; work progresses1/2 per tick. New application, treatment or expiry changes actual finish times, not text-only speed. Multiple slow status sources use an explicit scoped additive rule capped10; ordinary numeric modifier grants retain default stacking.

Treatment requires an actual active source, authorized physically reachable target, exact matching finite ingredient, known3AP/1s and known dose reduction. Expended ingredients remain spent through failed access/permission changes. Treatment reduces future dose; it does not undo committed injury or restore a life. Healing is a distinct funded/ingredient-backed transaction with bounded severity reduction, not a cure, resurrection, consciousness repair or player-life recovery. No NPC/Agent/Azimah receives player lives by analogy.

Boundary order is explicitly proposed: elapsed DOT over the prior half-open interval; control/expiry and due status/application/treatment/healing; sensing then reaction/ordinary completions; swept contact effects; recovery ticks. A completed sweep is evaluated geometrically with actual relative trajectories. Physical readiness additionally uses exact contact time. This discrete ordering is not a claim of general continuous physiology. F87 never lets healing reverse an already committed100 incapacity threshold; actual death/life transactions remain separate policy-bound systems. Interval damage precedes boundary healing; later same-tick contact effects are a distinct scheduled stage. Injury recovery uses actual current severity through F35/F37 at each one-second recovery tick, preserving chunking and shared EA allocation.

Modifiers, history and acquisition

Each grant has typed source, subject/operand/scope/tags, flat/factor, conditions, start/end, priority/exclusions and actual committed provenance. All applicable distinct sources stack by default. One actual causal source cannot be copied through aliases to stack twice. Existing exclusions, priorities and caps remain explicit scoped exceptions. F88 replaces the base operand first, then applies all selected flats/factors and the final cap: replacing10 by20 with+2 gives22; no replacement with+3 and×2 caps at25, not26.

Suppression temporarily suspends active grant capability/eligibility without ending ownership. Nullification neutralizes its numeric contribution but leaves grant identity active. Revocation ends the grant permanently from that time, retaining history. Replacement changes a specified operand base, not source identity or other applicable sources. Ending suppression/nullification does not resurrect a revoked grant. Tags, predicates and end-exclusive expiry are checked at actual use; conditional grant facts follow actual prior committed grants, not rumors. Temporary attribute/skill changes affect real derived execution prerequisites, not permanent learned acquisition.

Attempt/prepared, cancellation/failure, resisted/no-effect and actual success are distinct committed outcomes. Deliberate cancellation does not manufacture a qualifying success/failure reward. Evidence predicates count actual committed actor-filtered causal history, not claims or another actor's events. Eligibility is separate from actual acquisition: a permitted explicit policy, knowledge, non-sacrificed family and finite fee are still required, with no automatic universal reward or practitioner-Tier gate. NPC ordinary entities can use the same history/modifier contracts; the broad autonomous NPC scheduler remains I08.

Previous checkpoint — I05/I06 bounded production and advancement checkpoint

PROPOSAL. All18 overall IN PROGRESS. Exact saved creator t99:380–419,570–690,849–864 and fixed constraints at237–376/423–494 were re-read, together with t100:94–163. Current creator directions and source boundaries

The 12:38 CST creator deferral keeps affinity names and weakness/resistance relationships UNKNOWN. A–E are opaque fixture domains, not five finalized roster entries. Conduit affinity binding BLOCKS; no missing triangle becomes neutral or hides inside a material tag. Other actual technique/Agent/body profiles and custody/return/consumption policies remain UNKNOWN. No source retrieval, repeated roster question, Kernel/repository/remote/connected record, art or routine change occurred.

t104/t105 exact parent relays remain qualitative setting compatibility: industrial/urban/nearly-modern gate/dungeon production alongside mystical/ceremonial/locally indigenous cultures and pre20th-century-inspired clothing. God Eater/Fate reference cinematic motion, impact and power differences, not imported feats/lore. The focus is ONE Pangaea-like continent in a much larger world; Hunter×Hunter supplies scale feel, not geography. Bench/furnace and local100m test frames are not planetary dimensions or a completed industrial economy. Broad calibration remains I10; locked painterly art is unchanged.

One integrated authoritative coordinator

ProductionBook extends TemporalBook, which extends the same LifecycleBook/ResourceBook/ObjectGraph/AuthorityBook coordinator. It does not introduce an infinite materials counter, parallel resource balance or free clock. Two no-op temporal extension hooks leave prior workflows unchanged; this subclass advances funded production/ritual work at actual boundaries, under actual slow/prohibition/injury and current permission/access conditions.

Starting work requires a registered immutable typed recipe/profile, actual permanent/learned state, permission, knowledge, real owned held input leaves, a physically held durable permitted tool and an authorized functioning reachable facility. Material identities must be distinct, nonliving, unencumbered, not consumed/reserved/ammunition/Heirlooms, at least0.1kg each, and have known density/mass/volume/provenance. The tool/facility cannot be consumed as its own input. Legal ownership and possession remain distinct: a title alone does not retrieve inputs, original, blueprint, tool or Agent token.

AP and per-accessible-domain EA are reserved through the existing escrow ledger. Preparation is actually debited; inputs become zero-mass historical tombstones and one physical WIP object with their exact summed mass. Conversion happens before WIP creation, avoiding transient double carried load at an exactly full container/body. Work pays finite AP/EA each tick as1/slowFactor work-seconds, not a client qualified flag. The actor cannot rest or perform incompatible ordinary work while reserved.

Paid work and tool wear commit before the output transaction. If output validation fails, completed exertion remains spent; no refund of the last attempted manufacturing interval. Failure/cancellation refunds only unspent escrow, keeps sacrifice loss, turns WIP into recoverable physical scrap at its ACTUAL current location and releases edition/tool/token reservations. Failed WIP does not teleport to the legal owner. Unknown policies or missing prerequisites never become successful defaults.

Complete finite tag and production registry

Registry contains15 proposed mechanical tags, three connector branches,12 category descriptors and12 concrete recipe fixtures. All12 prior material profiles in data/materials.json also have an explicit typed bridge retaining their density/stress/heat/service/binding/provenance fields. Native binding contributions apply once per distinct material-profile ID, not per input fragment. Legacy labels without a new operator remain declared descriptors: they do not grant optical transparency, ignition, fluid conversion or affinity authority. Unmapped labels/unknown batch grade-quality-floor policy block; material adaptation never invents a universal recipe.

This is a finite worked reference set, not a claim that the creator supplied a universal recipe/material catalogue. Tag identities bind typed operators:

  • Composition: metal/fiber/ceramic provide explicit compatibility bonuses/penalties.
  • Form: rigid and flexible conflict.
  • Capability/resistance: conductive and insulating conflict; brittle penalizes binding. Conductivity does not grant magic or an affinity.
  • Status/binding: hot requires actual heated processing; fiber+hot conflicts. Wet conflicts with sealed, and sealed requires actual dry conditions.
  • Origin/exclusion: excluded living-origin material cannot bind with living origin.
  • Synergy: annealed requires metal, actual annealed facility conditions and quality≥60.
  • Precision: explicit quality≥70 and rarity≥1 prerequisites; no descriptive name grants precision by itself.

Hard requires/forbids/conditions/quality/rarity gates precede numeric compatibility. Selected identities apply once in stable order; high rarity or quality cannot overpower a hard conflict. F97 then uses base+bonuses−penalties+(quality−50)/10+2×rarity, bounded0..100, threshold60. Mechanical processing uses that contract. Thermal additionally requires actual heated facility≥500K and every input service limit. Conduit binding remains source-blocked. Tests execute every one of the32,768 subsets of the15-tag registry, plus pair order and explicit condition/temperature cases.

Categories organize equipment, consumables, materials, components, containers, structures, vehicles, documents, valuables, relics, living objects and environmental objects. The same conserved physical production contract applies to supported nonliving outputs. Category names do not invent vehicle propulsion, currencies with guaranteed legal acceptance, organism creation or arbitrary infrastructure physics. Untyped living-object manufacture blocks; separately typed conscious Heirloom creation is supported. Existing lower-level geometry/fit/payload/connector rules remain real; full support/fracture/fluid/chemical/organism physics is outside this finite domain.

Grade, natural rarity, creation modifiers, quality and condition

These are independent operands—not synonyms or a single power score:

  1. Crafting Tier is an explicit recipe/license gate, not the practitioner's universal technique Tier.
  2. Starting grade is a recipe value. F89 adds weakest raw material grade and distinct registered creation modifiers, then enforces grade0..9−craftTier.
  3. Rarity floor/ceiling are separate known promises. F91 takes maximum floors and minimum recipe/grade ceiling. Impossible floor>ceiling rejects, never silently promotes.
  4. Natural rarity uses existing F23's corrected integer weights totaling100, including the odd-bonus first-weight remainder. F100 provides a bounded rejection-mapped deterministic bucket; natural outcome remains recorded before final floor/ceiling clamp.
  5. Creation modifier requires a typed known source and an ACTUAL once-used paid qualified Lifecycle activation. The single-output inlay fixture changes grade/floor only; it does not create True Damage or conceptual authority. Real True Damage records remain unverified; that creator example is not an automatic rarity bonus.
  6. Quality uses mass-weighted raw material quality (F98), clipped learned Craft skill, explicit flaw and attained quality ceiling (F90). Manufactured grade cannot become a free better raw grade on recycling.
  7. Condition is current wear, independent of quality/rarity/grade. It limits usable yield and intrinsic energy (F99); the rest of physical mass remains scrap, not vanished matter. New condition is explicitly declared; production is not assumed automatically perfect.

Random rarity-dependent binding is checked AFTER paid irreversible preparation; otherwise a failed high-rarity tag check would be an unfunded lottery probe. Known impossible profiles still reject before spending. Physical roots/design/craftTier seed the deterministic fixture draw; request/output renaming or cancellation does not reroll the lot. New composition requires finite actual material, including the0.1kg ingredient minimum. Hash-uniform sampling is a stated mathematical fixture assumption, not a deployed private-entropy/commit-reveal service or a canonical luck rule.

For example, raw grade2+starting2 at crafting Tier0 yields grade4; recipe rarity ceiling4 becomes effective ceiling2. Raw quality80 with capped skill100 yields quality90, independently of the draw.3kg input for a2kg output leaves1kg scrap. From100AP/100EA(A), Tier0's explicit4/5 work-AP factor gives preparation2AP+work2×4/5AP, ending482/5AP and98EA(A), with two work-seconds. These are proposed fixture numbers, not established body statistics.

Production modes, standards and restricted families

Normal production has one output; batch production has2..8, with linear finite material/work/EA/AP accounting, distinct physical identities and no free quantity. Limited editions have a registered immutable design/cap; preparation reserves every output slot. completed+reserved≤cap is atomic under two-crafter races. Cancellation releases uncompleted slots but retains prep/material/work loss; two simultaneous completions cannot exceed the cap or rebind its identity.

A registered original establishes a design/crafting-Tier standard with its actual intrinsic capability envelope and rarity ceiling. Later output at that crafting Tier must fit the whole componentwise envelope, including quality-adjusted intrinsic energy, range, duration, targets, information depth and explicitly empty ordinary authority operations. Rarity is not a summed substitute for that bound. A higher-quality later copy cannot silently exceed the original. No second original replaces the first at the same design/crafting Tier; higher-tier standards need their own explicit permission/profile, not a name edit.

Blueprint creation requires actual inspected permitted physical access to the registered ordinary original. The resulting document contains a provenance-bound capability template; the DOCUMENT does not gain the original's intrinsic energy or authority. A replica requires the real held blueprint, source original access, matching design/craftTier and no envelope excess. Forged/rebound payloads, remote titles and copies of restricted Heirlooms reject.

The three Heirloom families use the EXISTING irreversible family transactions inside this full production contract. Sacrificial preparation permanently removes the canonicalized family/aliases/variants even through later failure. Conscious creation makes a new identity; break ends it. Fragments gain raw-material provenance, so full-recipe reassembly can make a NEW conscious identity, never restore the ended one. Agent creation still requires the actual typed Agent/concept/embodiment profile, singleton reservation, one Heirloom and one fixed assigned Tier; manifested Creator identity grants no Agent-creation privilege. No rarity/grade promotion grants absent authority. Heirlooms can establish protected standards without making ordinary blueprint/replica clones legal.

Ordinary repair is separately paid/ingredient-backed surface reconditioning, not replacement of missing physical mass/components. F93 lowers the persistent repair ceiling by1, then restores at most the known finite amount below that ceiling. It changes neither quality nor rarity nor grade. Actual consumed repair matter becomes spent scrap; no infinite perfect repair or conscious-identity resurrection. Tool durability uses existing F69 on cumulative wear, integer-rounded once against starting condition, not independently rounded every slow tick. Actual physical and resource-book tool conditions agree.

Eligibility versus committed advancement

The nine transition profiles use the existing proposed ten-Tier threshold table, with explicit synthetic A–E family maps/quotas—not creator-approved affinity names or counts. Initial Tier9 is a labeled baseline, not a fake completed ritual. For each transition, eligibility is distinct from preparation and from committed Tier change.

Candidate state derives from immutable initialized permanent attributes/learned skills and actually acquired canonicalized families. Required prior families, forbidden/incompatible acquired families, actual affinity access and technique-specific/affinity mastery are checked. Each actual qualifying affinity must meet its own explicit unique-family quota. Alias names do not multiply counts. Inactive but attained techniques can count toward progression; that does NOT mean they are currently executable. Temporary buffs cannot substitute for permanent milestone attributes; knowing a higher-Tier technique is not acquisition, eligibility or execution. Ordinary execution keeps no universal practitioner-Tier gate.

Conceptual-development evidence is an explicit PROPOSAL authorized review of DISTINCT actual successful paid manufacturing events. Rumor, cancellation, failed work, another actor's history and duplicate causes do not count. This finite review fixture is not a claim that manufacturing automatically defines the setting's whole conceptual-development system; I07 broader development remains open.

The ritual requires actual authorized reachable facility, positive equipped physical ritual instrument, known consented actor request, exact real material mass and an intact physically possessed permitted Agent Heirloom whose actual Agent/profile/Heirloom assigned Tier matches the target. Fixture custody is established by a SEPARATE Agent embodiment's real physical relinquishment under the explicit retained-inert policy, not a forged attestation on the candidate body. Ownership need not transfer merely because possession did. Canonical acquisition/consumption/return policy stays UNKNOWN; no automatic recall or Heirloom consumption occurs.

Preparation reserves real AP/per-domain EA and the token/tool, consumes material into physical WIP and pays prep. Work follows actual slow/prohibition/current prerequisites and equipped instrument condition; lost mastery/material access/token/equipment/permission/site or authoritative failure prevents completion. Failed/cancelled rituals retain spent costs/matter without Tier change or unapproved injury/life/skill loss. Successful work then rechecks everything, grants the next Tier exactly once and appends permanent benefit provenance. One-step-only is an explicit fixture policy; skipping or unknown policy blocks.

Semantic correction checkpoint — explicit partial independent completion

PROPOSAL. All18 overall IN PROGRESS. I06 remains OPEN for F08; I07/I08 remain unfinished. This is a corrective checkpoint before resuming development/autonomy, not a claim that every requested independent mechanic is complete.

Why earlier acceptance was reopened

The independent report found eight implementation violations and two contract gaps despite923 passing tests. Its evidence is diagnostic, not creator canon or implementation authorization. The original t99/t100 assignment supplies the authoring scope. Earlier collective “I01–I06 bounded acceptance covered” statements were too broad and are superseded here. I02 family-only invariants remain supported; its production integration uses the corrected I05 salvage path.

Corrected mechanisms and numerical consequences

  • F01 — ritual site/instrument validation: separate the profile-mismatch conditional from actual facility/tool calls. Admission, every work tick and final commitment now require current reachable authorized intact site and actual authorized intact equipped instrument. Six pre-admission defects reject without spend; a site broken after one paid tick fails without Tier gain. In that worked9→8 fixture, remaining AP92/EA(A)96 preserves prep and one tick, versus the old unearned Tier grant.
  • F02 — repertoire versus milestone evidence: iterate acquired canonical families and select only validated attained families contributing to the actual qualifying affinities. Permanent prerequisites, prior/forbidden families and relevant mastery still apply to each selected contributor; inactivity alone does not erase attainment. Unknown/unqualified/unrelated extra acquisitions do not poison sufficient genuine quota evidence. Unknown or invalid contributors cannot supply a missing quota. The former prerequisite test is retained with exactly four A contributors, making the defective contributor necessary; this corrects its unintended universal-all-repertoire expectation, not its prerequisite enforcement assertion.
  • F03 — scoped replacement: replacement candidates now retain their grant identity and must survive the same current tag/predicate/exclusion/nullify/suppress selection as flats/factors. Highest priority then stable operator identity selects among survivors. A base10 queried outside the +2 grant's scope remains10 with selected=[], not50; inside scope the explicit replacement50 plus2 resolves52.
  • F04 — current original standard: saved output rarity is checked again against the current committed design/crafting-Tier standard. A reserved rarity2 product cannot commit after a rarity0 original appears. Completion fails, leaves physical scrap and retains all paid work; no late reroll, favorable clamp or new output identity. The four-work-second Tier0 fixture ends474/5AP, with the original saved rarity2 draw still recorded.
  • F05 — physical True Damage: positive FlightProfile.truth now requires a typed registered TrueContactProfile, linked to the activated technique, actual round kind, positive maximum, supported anatomical region, minimum relative speed and permission. Unbacked descriptive positive truth rejects at admission. Actual swept contact and qualification enable the component; missing/changing qualification blocks instead of silently erasing accepted damage. Ordinary and True components remain separate through paid affinity ward composition. A fully energy-blocked ordinary round with qualified True10 produces injury10 in the tolerance100 fixture. Mixed ordinary20/True10 resolves20×actualWardFactor+10, not30×factor. This remains the finite torso/axis domain, not arbitrary anatomy or universal conceptual authority.
  • F06 — actual durability: paid technique wear atomically synchronizes the physical object and reservoir condition. Any contradictory existing views block authoritative transactions; production tools validate agreement. One wear point now yields99 in BOTH views, including on replay; broken/mismatched reserved equipment cannot activate or secretly remain usable in another subsystem.
  • F07 — failed delivery: pending application resolution snapshots the already-funded state and reconciles target-side transfer, resistance and status together. A0.02kg compound that exceeds remaining0.01kg payload capacity fails with no status and resistance still1, not0. Actor AP/consumed compound remain spent and a distinct committed failure is recorded. This is not free treatment, refunded preparation or partial exposure invented after the fact.
  • F09 — independent ownership: TechniqueActor has explicit owned_affinities and ownership_provenance, distinct from access/active sets. Existing generic execution still depends on access/qualification, not a universal ownership/Tier gate. Milestone derivation requires known independent ownership and provenance; borrowed A access cannot certify A ownership. Production fixtures explicitly declare each opaque domain's ownership; defaults remain UNKNOWN, never access-as-owned.
  • F10 — mass-weighted salvage: actual source masses are captured BEFORE input tombstones, for manufacturing/family work and rituals. All scrap routes use F98 with those masses, retain root/contribution lineage, and apply the existing one-point salvage penalty.9kg quality0+0.1kg quality100 yields raw100/91, then scrap quality0, not49. Ritual2.9kg quality0+0.1kg quality100 yields raw10/3 and scrap quality2. Success remainder, cancellation, failure and repeated salvage are exercised; conserved mass cannot launder bulk quality.

AO Chessboard Elemental Axes — Proposal

Status: PROPOSED / NOT CANON. This Chessboard-facing framework does not overwrite or resolve the earlier source-access UNKNOWN affinity roster or resistance/weakness records.

Axis I — Eight-element cycle

Fire → Wood → Earth → Lightning → Water → Ice → Wind → Metal → Fire. Each element is advantageous against the next two in the cycle and disadvantaged against the previous two; six cycle matchups are neutral. This circular model does not mean one element is universally stronger.

Draft matchup mapping

ElementAdvantageous againstDisadvantaged against
FireWood, EarthWind, Metal
WoodEarth, LightningFire, Metal
EarthLightning, WaterFire, Wood
LightningWater, IceWood, Earth
WaterIce, WindEarth, Lightning
IceWind, MetalLightning, Water
WindMetal, FireWater, Ice
MetalFire, WoodIce, Wind

This is a balanced draft mapping; thematic validation and combat effects remain open.

Axis II — Light and Shadow

Light and Shadow occupy their own special axis, outside the eight-element cycle. Their polarity does not automatically establish universal victory or defeat. Their techniques interact based on specific properties. Interaction with the eight cycle elements is technique-specific until defined.

Combat resolution boundaries

Matchups may inform technique effectiveness but do not automatically guarantee hits, multipliers, immunity, DR bypass, penetration, or Break. They remain distinct from AP, EA, DR, damage resistance, penetration, Break, Tier, technique properties, proficiency, terrain, and positioning. No numerical coefficients are established here.

Racial development and composites

Races have natural elemental affinity and progression in use/charge; some may possess innate racial techniques. Keep affinity, progression, charge behavior, racial technique, and EA distinct. Composite elements may emerge from combinations or interactions, but recipes, classification, matchups, and mechanics are separate future definitions. No race-element assignments, thresholds, costs, or technique counts are specified.

Full proposal: mechanics/20-chessboard-elemental-axes-proposal.md.
